=== KEY FINDINGS ===
F1 ReliefWeb holds NO Niger cholera count, death count, dated presence statement or explicit zero for any period inside
   2026-07-27..2026-11-16.
F2 Health Cluster lead RESOLVED: ReliefWeb has Niger 'Bulletin mensuel du Cluster-Santé' N1 (9 Apr), N2 (22 May), N3 (17 Jul) and
   N4 (31 Aug 2026; cholera data to W30 = 26 Jul, already AI rows 47-53, src 43) ONLY. N5/N6 do not exist on ReliefWeb as of
   2026-11-17. They are unpublished, not just blocked. N4 has no figure or presence statement dated on or after 27 Jul (its
   narrative covers May-July; cholera table 'à la semaine 30').
F3 OEW lead RESOLVED: ReliefWeb copies of WHO AFRO Weekly Bulletin on Outbreaks weeks 31-41 (data to 11 Oct 2026) were read.
   Every all-events table lists Niger only for diphtheria (755/9 at 26 Jul -> 949/36 at 20 Sep), hepatitis E (80/2 -> 215/18 at
   4 Oct; Téra 213, Diffa 2), measles (10,379/16 to 26 Jul) and the humanitarian crisis. No week has a Niger cholera event. WHO AFRO
   never listed Niger's 2026 Diffa cholera event. That is a non-listing, NOT a zero.
F4 Africa CDC EIR (issue date 13-Sep-2026, file '24-September'): cholera 119,052 cases / 1,893 deaths from 20 AU MS (AGO BDI CMR TCD
   CAF COG COD ETH KEN MWI MOZ NAM NGA RWA SOM SSD SDN TZA ZMB ZWE). Niger is absent, appearing only under diphtheria (78; 6).
   This matches the main pass's 25-Oct issue. Non-listing, NOT a zero.
F5 OCHA West and Central Africa 'Latest events at a glance' with a Niger item (11-17 Aug, 1-7 Sep, 15-21 Sep, 6-12 Oct, 20-26 Oct):
   Niger items are funding, floods (34,372 affected at 1 Sep; 57,356 at 14 Sep), displacement and an IDP-site fire in N'Guigmi
   (21 Oct). No cholera. Their cholera items concern CMR, TCD, DRC and NGA.
F6 Pre-window corroboration (NOT added; scope rule): UNHCR Niger Q2 operational update (reliefweb 4227592): 'five suspected cases
   identified in Diffa (29 June-3 July)'. This is consistent with AI rows 47-48 (W26 2 + W27 3).
F7 Context only: IFRC Chad DREF ops update MDRTD026 (4229793) reports a cross-border cholera coordination meeting of CMR, CAF, NER,
   NGA and TCD held with IFRC support, but gives no Niger figures. OCHA Chad Lac sitrep 30 Sep (4232682) reports 802 cases / 46 deaths
   in Lac and mentions Niger only for border insecurity. KSrelief-WHO Lake Chad cholera agreement (4231251) covers Niger and gives
   no count.
F8 UNICEF Niger HSR No.1 (Jan-Jun 2026, dated 30 Jul; image-only, rendered) mentions cholera only as a seasonal risk and announces
   'Next sitrep: October 2026'. That sitrep is not on ReliefWeb as of 17 Nov.
